# Every platformer: illustrated media atlas

One entry per stable identity. Artwork and videos retain their exact official source. A source link without a stream is an honest gap, not an invented playable video. GitHub Markdown links video; the interactive library supplies players where supported.

### [Super Meat Boy Forever](Game-581660-Super-Meat-Boy-Forever)

[![Super Meat Boy Forever](https://shared.akamai.steamstatic.com/store_item_assets/steam/apps/581660/header.jpg?t=1641929185)](https://store.steampowered.com/app/581660/)
